77 issues for the Must scope, created milestone by milestone (Q17), plus [#2711](https://github.com/fluentcms/FluentCMS/issues/2711) for the deferred PostgreSQL and SQL Server validation (Q19), which has no milestone until the code is complete. Should items get issues when their milestone is planned, not now. M6 was planned with twelve issues where the 77 counted seven, and M7, which the 77 do not count, with the sixteen of 9.8.

## 10. Open questions and suggestions

All eighteen questions were decided on 1 Nov 2026, and Q19 was added the same day after approval. The table keeps each question and its decision; where the decision differs from the recommendation that was made, it says so. Section 10.3 holds the decisions taken when M6 was planned on 12 Nov 2026, and section 10.4 those of M7, planned the same day after M6 was merged.

### 10.1 Decisions on the open questions

| Id | Question | Decision (1 Nov 2026) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q1 | Package granularity: one `FluentCMS.Core` holding engine, REST and identity, or split `FluentCMS.Core`, `FluentCMS.Api`, `FluentCMS.Identity`? | One `FluentCMS.Core` plus data providers. The existing `FluentCMS.Identity` 0.0.5 ID is unlisted and deprecated per decision 61. |
| Q2 | How does a Docker-only user define content types? The image cannot load a DLL (decision 11) and types are code-first. | The fluent builder loads a `schema.json` mounted into the image. Schema in a file under version control is not a UI builder (1.24). Code-first stays primary; the sample pack stays for evaluation. |
| Q3 | Argon2id (4.2) versus Identity's PBKDF2 default; 4.32 says Identity primitives only. | PBKDF2 behind the replaceable hasher. Argon2id as an optional package later if asked. Row 4.2 is amended. |
| Q4 | Member login: OAuth 2.1 has no password grant. Hosted pages only, or also a first-party direct login? | Both: hosted pages by default, and an opt-in `member_password` custom grant for first-party apps, documented as the less secure option. |
| Q5 | EF model from property bags (runtime) or from source-generated row classes (compile time)? | Property bags; spike B-10 validates them on all three providers before the content tables are built. The spike confirmed property bags on SQLite, the only provider verified so far, and [ADR 0001](../decisions/0001-storage-spike-outcome.md) records the outcome and the resulting changes to this document. |
| Q6a | Core versus multi-site plugin boundary for site and locale management. | Sites and locales CRUD in core; hostnames, cross-site copy and per-site storage in the plugin. |
| Q6b | Single components flattened to columns, or JSON for all components? | Flatten single components to prefixed columns; JSON for repeatable ones. |
| Q7 | Block-field filtering depth across three providers (2.4 makes it Must). | Block-type presence and scalar paths one level deep, identical on all providers. Deeper paths deferred, PostgreSQL-only when they come. |
| Q8 | ImageSharp is under the Six Labors Split License (free for open source and small companies, paid above a revenue threshold). Keep it as the default `IImageProcessor`? | **ImageSharp stays the default, as the specification says.** The recommendation to evaluate SkiaSharp or NetVips was not taken. The documentation carries a licence note; the slot stays replaceable. As built (B-65): the default is SkiaSharp (MIT), since ImageSharp 4.x and 3.2 fail a Release build without a Six Labors license key and 3.1.12 has six open advisories; ImageSharp stays possible in the slot. |
| Q9 | New dependencies: OpenIddict, HotChocolate, ModelContextProtocol, Microsoft.Extensions.AI and providers, Scalar.AspNetCore, Cronos, MailKit, AWSSDK.S3, Azure.Storage.Blobs, Testcontainers, ImageSharp. | Approved in principle; each is named again in the issue that introduces it. ImageSharp was replaced by SkiaSharp (MIT) in B-65 (Q8). B-68 pins AWSSDK.S3 4.0.104.11, Azure.Storage.Blobs 12.30.1, Testcontainers.Minio 4.15.0 and Testcontainers.Azurite 4.16.0. Email ships as SMTP through MailKit plus a log-only sender for Development. |
| Q10 | GitHub and Apple member login need the community `AspNet.Security.OAuth.Providers` package. | Accepted (Apache 2.0, aspnet-contrib). |
| Q11 | Dynamic client registration policy for MCP clients. | Approval required by default; open-with-rate-limit as a setting. |
| Q12 | Record implementation-time architecture decisions as ADRs? | Yes, short ADRs in `docs/decisions/`, only for choices not already in the decisions log. |
| Q13 | Test assertion library. | Shouldly. |
| Q14 | Response shape: system fields flat with reserved names, or under a `sys` object? | Flat with reserved names. |
| Q15 | Client generation from OpenAPI. | Own TypeScript emitter; the C# generator (Kiota or own) decided in B-93. |
| Q16 | The uploaded `research/` folder names v2 and a possible paid tier; the public `main` must not mention v2. | Commit `specification/` and this document only. `research/` stays out of the repository and out of the planning folder; this document no longer refers to it. |
| Q17 | Issue granularity. | The 77 issues of section 9 under seven milestone tracking issues, created milestone by milestone, M0 and M1 first. |
| Q18 | Is the tooling part of this backend batch? | Yes. `FluentCMS.Testing` and the `migrations add` command sit in M1 because the core tests need them; the rest is M6 in this batch. |
| Q19 | Database providers during implementation: SQLite only locally, or PostgreSQL and SQL Server in CI from M0? | **SQLite only, locally and in CI, until the backend code is complete.** PostgreSQL and SQL Server validation and the completion of their provider packages are deferred to [#2711](https://github.com/fluentcms/FluentCMS/issues/2711). The recommendation to run the provider matrix in CI from M0 was not taken. Risk accepted: JSON filtering, job claiming, partial unique indexes and migration generation stay unverified on the primary production provider until then; issues #2677, #2681 and #2689 carry scope notes. Ended on 8 Nov 2026: #2711 runs the data tests on PostgreSQL 15 and SQL Server 2019 and 2025 in CI. |

### 10.2 Suggestions

| Id | Suggestion | Why |
| --- | --- | --- |
| S1 | Make `OperationContext` and `IAuthorizationEvaluator` the first two SDK types written and never let HTTP, GraphQL or MCP bypass the service (architecture test). | Every differentiator in the research (origin tagging, agent safety, one permission model) depends on it. |
| S2 | Run the storage spike (B-10) before any content-table code; time-box it to one week and record the result as an ADR. | Decision 19 says SQL Server JSON must be verified; the property-bag choice shapes everything in M1. |
| S3 | One polymorphic `cms_reference` table instead of a `_rels` table per type. | Media usage, reference integrity and delete warnings become one indexed query; polymorphic relations (Should) cost nothing later. |
| S4 | A type-independent `cms_document` state table. | Cross-type lists, trash, scheduling views and the MCP `query` tool need it; it keeps the state machine out of generated tables. |
| S5 | Outbox for events that feed webhooks, activity and cache invalidation. | Signed, retried webhooks are a stated differentiator; without an outbox a crash after commit loses the event. |
| S6 | Ship `FluentCMS.Testing` in M1, not M6. | The core's own integration tests are the first consumer; plugin authors inherit a proven harness. |
| S7 | Public API analyzers and package validation on `FluentCMS.Sdk` from day one. | Decision 14 promises semver; the build should enforce it. |
| S8 | `TimeProvider` everywhere, no `DateTimeOffset.UtcNow`. | Scheduling, leases, retention and token expiry are all testable without waiting. |
| S9 | Treat the hosted authorization pages as a backend deliverable with their own issue (B-43). | They are easy to forget because they are UI, and nothing signs in without them. |
| S10 | Reserve `/admin`, `/media`, `/mcp`, `/graphql`, `/connect`, `/health`, `/openapi`, `/scalar` in the route map now. | Hosts mount FluentCMS next to their own routes; a published list avoids collisions later. |
| S11 | Add a `Seed` API to the SDK in M1 and use it for the sample pack, tests and the template. | 11.5 is Must and three consumers need the same thing. |
| S12 | The research folder stays a private planning input outside the repository (Q16). | The restart announcement avoided mentioning v2, and the research text does. |

### 10.3 Decisions of the M6 planning

M6 was planned on 12 Nov 2026, after M0 to M5 were merged. The review of the plan against the code found work that the specification promised and section 9.7 did not list, and design points that 4.14 and 6 left open. Each was decided that day; the table says what a decision replaces.

| Id | Point | Decision (12 Nov 2026) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P1 | How do project commands reach the host's services, configuration and plugins? The tool loaded the host's assembly beside its own Core, which gave it a `DbContext` only and bound both to one version. | Commands run inside the host's process, which the tool starts. The reference host accepts the same commands, so an init container runs `fluentcms migrate` from the image. `migrations add` and `migrations remove` stay in the tool. |
| P2 | Command-line parser for about thirty commands. | System.CommandLine 2.0, referenced by the tool only. |
| P3 | Authentication to a running instance. | A management API key in `--token` or `FLUENTCMS_TOKEN`. An interactive login comes after 1.0. |
| P4 | A host whose types are data cannot run on PostgreSQL or SQL Server (`FCMS3154`); 4.3 expected the CLI to generate migrations from a schema file. | `fluentcms schema apply` compares the schema with the content tables and applies the difference, or writes it as SQL. Nothing changes a server database at start, which keeps decision 55, and no migration files are generated from a schema file. |
| P5 | Renames: a field renamed without `[FormerNames]` is a drop and an add (#2890). | `schema diff` and `migrations add` detect a possible rename and suggest the attribute; nothing is rewritten. A destructive change needs `--allow-destructive`. |
| P6 | `backup` was an export package with media and had no `restore`; a package leaves out users, keys and the activity log. | `backup` is an online snapshot of a SQLite database with the local media files, and `restore` puts it back. Server databases are documented, and the commands refuse there. |
| P7 | Export and import as jobs with a download need a place for the package that every instance reaches, and core has none. | Both are streamed within the request. No transfer job and no stored package. |
| P8 | The exit criteria say "on a fresh machine". | A CI job runs the sequence from packages in a folder feed on clean runners. A preview on nuget.org is a release decision of its own. |
| P9 | C# generator, Kiota or own (Q15), and who builds the clients that generated code needs (1.1). | Own emitters for C# and TypeScript from the schema snapshot, the C# one shared with the source generator. Minimal `FluentCMS.Client` and `@fluentcms/client` are built in M6 and not published; the frontend work adds the rest. |
| P10 | How does an import write, and what does it trigger? `CreateAsync` always mints an id (#2890). | Through the content service as the system, in an import mode that keeps ids, times and state; the create endpoints keep minting ids. A new `Origin.Import` marks what an import writes. Validation, hooks, cache eviction and the activity log run; webhooks are delivered only when the import asks for them. |
| P11 | Data that plugins own is not in a package, and adding it after 1.0 needs an SDK contract. | `ITransferContributor` goes into the SDK now. The media plugin implements it; the other first-party plugins follow in a minor release. |
| P12 | What is left out of M6? | The reflection-free mapper, the analyzer for obsolete SDK members and standalone binaries of the tool wait until after 1.0. The `mcp` proxy stays in M6. |
| P13 | What surrounds M6? | The four bugs of the M5 review (#3001, #3002, #3006, #3010) run beside M6. Its other follow-ups form a hardening milestone after M6, and then the admin gets its technical specification. Search and the Redis pack stay Should. |
| P14 | Rows 11.5 and 11.3 name a seed command and an MSBuild target that 6.1 did not have. | `fluentcms seed` is added. No MSBuild target runs `generate`: the source generator covers C# in a host, and a front end calls the command from its own build. Row 11.3 is amended. |
| P15 | The compose file of the reference host (4.16) was put off until #2711 and the media plugin. | A small issue of M6, with a migrate step, used by CI as a smoke test on PostgreSQL and MinIO. |

### 10.4 Decisions of the M7 planning

M7 was planned on 12 Nov 2026, after M6 was merged. The document had one sentence about it (8; 10.3, P13). The review of the plan against the code and the feature specification found what that sentence left out: code that was never reviewed as a whole, a contract and a migration history that were about to be published as they stood, an admin row that needs a plugin tiered Should, and no way to publish. Each point was decided that day; the table says what a decision replaces.

| Id | Point | Decision (12 Nov 2026) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| H1 | What does M7 end with? P13 foresaw a hardening milestone and P8 left a preview on nuget.org as a release decision of its own. | A public preview, `1.0.0-preview.1`: the packages on nuget.org and the image of the reference host on a registry, pushed by a workflow from a tag. The contract stays free to break between previews: every entry stays in `PublicAPI.Unshipped.txt` and no package validation baseline is set until 1.0.0. The minimal `FluentCMS.Client` is published with the others, since generated C# compiles against it, which replaces "not published" of P9 for it. |
| H2 | The AI assist plugin holds three clients with three wire formats (5.5), and two of them never called a real endpoint. | OpenAI-compatible endpoints only: `openAi`, `azureOpenAi` and `openAiCompatible`, which share one client. The `Anthropic` and `OllamaSharp` packages go. Ollama is reached through its `/v1` endpoint, without a key, and any other client through the `IAiClientFactory` slot. Row 9.15 is amended. |
| H3 | Member sign-in and AI assist were tested against stubs only (#3007, #3008), and a real provider needs an account that only the maintainer can open. | Verified in M7: Google, Entra ID (for the Microsoft handler and generic OIDC) and GitHub for sign-in; one OpenAI-compatible endpoint with a key and a local Ollama for AI assist. Apple and Azure OpenAI are named as not verified in the notes of the preview and are done before 1.0.0. |
| H4 | The review of 7 Nov 2026 read the code up to M3 and the review of M5 its own; M4 and M6 were reviewed per pull request only. | One review of the code of M4 and M6 is the first issue of M7, with a security pass on `schema apply`, import, `restore`, the `mcp` proxy, the MCP confirmations and the GraphQL limits. High and Medium findings are fixed in M7; Low ones wait without a milestone. |
| H5 | About twenty system migrations and eight plugin migrations for each provider exist, and none was published. | They are squashed into one baseline for each stream and provider at the end of M7, on `main`. A database of the old chain is refused and recreated. Previews make no promise to upgrade a database until 1.0.0. |
| H6 | The admin's list views need free-text search (category 3, row 4, a Must), and `q=` is refused without the search plugin, a Should (P13). | A minimal search plugin moves into M7: full-text search on PostgreSQL, FTS5 on SQLite, a match on a part of the text on SQL Server, kept current from the entry events, with a rebuild job. Row 2.23 becomes a Must. Ranking, stemming by language, a full-text catalogue on SQL Server and an external index stay out. The Redis pack stays a Should. |
| H7 | `Principal.IsInGroup` answers false outside a row rule, which fails open for a check that denies by group, and taking a member out of a group needs `members.manage` only (#3004). | It throws outside a row rule. The operation's principal does not carry the groups, which would cost a query on every request of a member. Taking a member out of a group, and deleting a group, need the grants of the group's role, as adding does. |
| H8 | A site that saves settings without a secret gets a copy of the secret of the instance defaults, and the copy cannot be told from a secret of its own once the defaults change (#3005). | An inherited secret is not copied. A site that sets none reads the secret of the defaults at use, so a replaced key reaches every site that inherits it, and a plugin can ask whether a secret is the site's own. |
| H9 | #3011 asks for a note for an instance built on M3 that upgrades. | With one baseline (H5) and nothing published before it, that upgrade cannot occur, and the note falls away. The line in the startup report stays, for a host that turns `PerSite` back to `Shared`. |
| H10 | When is the technical specification of the admin written (P13: after the hardening milestone), and how is its work numbered? | Beside M7, as `docs/planning/admin-technical-spec.md`, with milestones of its own, A0 onward, and issues numbered `A-nn`. M7 carries an audit of the Management API against the admin's Must rows; a gap that changes a shape or a route is fixed in M7, before the preview. |
| H11 | Where does the work run? | On `feature/m7` with one merge into `main` after full CI, as M6 did. Bugs go to `main`. The baseline (H5) and the tag of the preview come last, on `main`. |
| H12 | What can no lane do? | The maintainer opens the development applications and the public origin for H3, reserves the `FluentCMS.` prefix on nuget.org (decision 61), sets up the publishing credentials and a `release` environment with a required reviewer, makes the image package public, and says whether the `@fluentcms` scope on npm is the project's; until it is, `@fluentcms/client` is attached to the GitHub release and not pushed to npm. |

## Appendix A: worked example

The `Article` type from 4.2 on PostgreSQL, prefix `cms_`.

```sql
-- shared, non-localized fields, one row per document and state
CREATE TABLE cms_article (
    id              uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    document_id     uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id         uuid NOT NULL,
    state           smallint NOT NULL,           -- 0 draft, 1 published
    category        varchar(64),
    published_on    timestamptz,
    video_url       varchar(2048),
    sections        jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',   -- [{"type": "hero", "id": "V1StGXR8_Z5j", ...}]
    seo_meta_title  varchar(70),
    seo_meta_description varchar(160),
    UNIQUE (document_id, state, site_id)
);

-- localized fields, one row per document, locale and state
CREATE TABLE cms_article_i18n (
    id              uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    document_id     uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id         uuid NOT NULL,
    locale          varchar(16) NOT NULL,
    state           smallint NOT NULL,
    title           varchar(200) NOT NULL,
    slug            varchar(200) NOT NULL,
    body            jsonb NOT NULL,
    UNIQUE (document_id, locale, state, site_id)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_cms_article_i18n_slug
    ON cms_article_i18n (site_id, locale, slug) WHERE state = 1;
CREATE INDEX ix_cms_article_category ON cms_article (site_id, state, category);
CREATE INDEX ix_cms_article_published_on ON cms_article (site_id, state, published_on);

-- author, tags and hero live in cms_reference, with locale '' (never NULL) for non-localized values:
-- (source_type='article', source_document_id, state, locale '', field='author', position 0,
--  target_type='author', target_id)
```

A Delivery request and response:

```http
GET /api/delivery/v1/article?filter[category][eq]=news&sort=-publishedOn&pageSize=2&populate=author&locale=fr-CA
Authorization: ApiKey fcms_dk_...
```

```json
{
  "items": [
    {
      "id": "0192f2a4-7b1c-7d2e-9f10-2a3b4c5d6e7f",
      "status": "published",
      "locale": "fr-CA",
      "version": 7,
      "publishedAt": "2026-11-01T10:41:00Z",
      "updatedAt": "2026-11-01T10:40:00Z",
      "createdAt": "2026-10-30T08:00:00Z",
      "title": "Bonjour",
      "slug": "bonjour",
      "category": "news",
      "publishedOn": "2026-11-01T00:00:00Z",
      "author": { "id": "0192f2a4-1111-7000-8000-000000000001", "type": "author", "name": "Amir" },
      "tags": [{ "id": "0192f2a4-2222-7000-8000-000000000002", "type": "tag" }],
      "hero": { "id": "0192f2a4-3333-7000-8000-000000000003", "type": "media" },
      "sections": [{ "type": "hero", "id": "V1StGXR8_Z5j", "heading": "Bienvenue", "image": { "id": "0192f2a4-3333-7000-8000-000000000003", "type": "media" } }],
      "seo": { "metaTitle": "Bonjour", "metaDescription": null },
      "fallbacks": { "body": "fr" }
    }
  ],
  "total": 41,
```

## Appendix B: route map

| Prefix | Surface | Auth |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/delivery/v1` | Delivery API | none (Public role), delivery key, preview key, bearer with `draft` |
| `/api/management/v1` | Management API | bearer, management key |
| `/api/members/v1` | member registration and account | none for register, verify and reset, member bearer for `me` |
| `/connect/*`, `/.well-known/*` | OpenIddict, client registration and metadata | per endpoint |
| `/account/*` | hosted pages; the member pages under `/account/members` | the admin cookie, and the member cookie under `/account/members` |
| `/media/{id}/{filename}` | asset bytes and transforms | signature, or folder permission for private |
| `/graphql` | GraphQL plugin | same as REST |
| `/mcp` | MCP plugin | bearer with `mcp`, API key |
| `/health/live`, `/health/ready` | probes | none |
| `/openapi/v1.json`, `/scalar` | document and UI | configurable; mapped in Development, elsewhere with `FluentCms:OpenApi:Enabled` |
| `/admin` | reserved for the Blazor admin | |

A plugin mounts groups under `/api/delivery/v1` and `/api/management/v1` with `MapDelivery` and `MapManagement` (4.13), such as the media plugin's `GET /api/delivery/v1/media/{id}`, and its own groups elsewhere with `MapGroup` or `MapProtectedResource`, never under a prefix of core. All prefixes are relative to the configured `PathBase`. The version segment allows Delivery and Management to evolve independently; schema changes are never API versions (2.32).

## Appendix C: system table catalogue

| Table | Area |
| --- | --- |
| `cms_site`, `cms_site_locale` | sites and locales |
| `cms_document`, `cms_reference`, `cms_version`, `cms_schedule`, `cms_schema_history` | content and publishing |
| `cms_user`, `cms_user_role`, `cms_user_claim`, `cms_user_login`, `cms_user_token`, `cms_invitation`, `cms_role`, `cms_role_permission` | identity and authorization |
| `cms_oidc_application`, `cms_oidc_authorization`, `cms_oidc_scope`, `cms_oidc_token`, `cms_signing_key`, `cms_data_protection_key` | authorization server |
| `cms_api_key` | API keys |
| `cms_idempotency_key` | idempotency keys of the Management API |
| `cms_member`, `cms_member_login`, `cms_member_token`, `cms_member_group`, `cms_member_group_member` | members |
| `cms_activity` | activity log |
| `cms_job`, `cms_job_attempt`, `cms_recurring_job`, `cms_lock`, `cms_outbox` | jobs and infrastructure |
| `cms_setting`, `cms_plugin_site` | settings and plugins |
| `cms_media_asset`, `cms_media_folder`, `cms_media_variant`, `cms_media_preset` | media plugin |
| `cms_webhooks_subscription`, `cms_webhooks_delivery` | webhooks plugin |
| `cms_mcp_instruction`, `cms_mcp_prompt` | MCP plugin |
| `cms_ai_usage` | AI assist plugin |
| `cms_multi_site_host` | multi-site plugin |

Content type tables (`cms_{type}`, `cms_{type}_i18n`) are generated from the host's schema and owned by the host's migrations.
